Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a cycles algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 883. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 281/2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on cycles structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 883. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 281. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
